-- [dbo].[MobspDyUpdateFormFieldValues]            /*AI14010927 - Copied from web*/  /*AI14011011 - Check Values*/  CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspDyUpdateFormFieldValues]  (    /*    EXEC MobspDyUpdateFormFieldValues 97,N'Val3',N'',3,'2018-10-20','13970728',2,7,'dasti',1065   */  @FieldValueId INT,  @Value NVARCHAR(MAX),  @ValueLang NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,  @UserId INT,  @RegisterDate DATETIME,  @RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),  @Year1 SMALLINT,  @Month1 SMALLINT,  @AppType VARCHAR(20),  @LanguageID INT  )  AS  BEGIN  /**/  /********************************************************************************/  BEGIN TRY  /**/   IF(RIGHT(ISNULL(@Value,''), 1) = ']') /*AI14011011- Check Values*/   BEGIN   /**/    /****************************** Update Value ************************************/    UPDATE [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldValues]    SET      [Value] = @Value     ,[UserId] = @UserId     ,[RegisterDate] = @RegisterDate     ,[RegisterDatePersian] = @RegisterDatePersian     ,[Year1] = @Year1     ,[Month1] = @Month1     ,[AppType] = @AppType          WHERE [FormFieldValueId] = @FieldValueId        /******* Insert to lang table ********/    IF (ISNULL(@ValueLang, '') <> '' AND (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM OPENJSON(@ValueLang)) > 0)    BEGIN    /**/     DECLARE @LangId INT,@Val NVARCHAR(MAX)         /**/     DECLARE CrsrVal CURSOR LOCAL  FOR      SELECT            JS.LanguageId,       ISNULL(JS.[Value], '')      FROM OPENJSON(@ValueLang)      WITH (       [LanguageId] INT '$.LanguageId',       [Value] NVARCHAR(MAX) '$.Value'      ) AS JS      WHERE JS.LanguageID <> @LanguageID      AND RIGHT(ISNULL(JS.[Value],''), 1) = ']'/*AI14011011- Check Values*/     /**/     OPEN CrsrVal     FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrVal INTO @LangId, @Val     WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0     BEGIN     /**/      DECLARE @ValLangId INT = 0      SET @ValLangId = (SELECT TOP 1 [CrmDyFormFieldValueLangID] FROM [dbo].   [CrmDyFormFieldValues_Lang] WHERE [FieldValueId] = @FieldValueId AND [LanguageID] = @LangId)      IF(ISNULL(@ValLangId,0) > 0)      BEGIN /*Update*/       UPDATE [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldValues_Lang]          SET [Value] = @Val            WHERE [CrmDyFormFieldValueLangID] = @ValLangId      END      ELSE      BEGIN /*Insert*/       INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldValues_Lang]          ([FieldValueId]          ,[Value]          ,[LanguageID])       VALUES          (@FieldValueId          ,@Val          ,@LangId)      END                    /**/                FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrVal INTO @LangId, @Val     END     CLOSE CrsrVal     DEALLOCATE CrsrVal      /**/    END    /**/    SELECT 1 AS [Result]        /**/   END   ELSE IF(RIGHT(ISNULL(@Value,''), 1) <> ']')/*AI14011011- Check Values*/   BEGIN   /**/    SELECT -1 AS [Result]   /**/   END      /**/  END TRY  BEGIN CATCH  /**/   SELECT 0 AS [Result]    /**/  END CATCH  /********************************************************************************/  /**/  END              GO
-- [dbo].[MobspInsertAutoDocItemSerial]              /*AI14020613 - Copied from web*/  CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspInsertAutoDocItemSerial]  (   /*   EXEC MobspInsertAutoDocItemSerial    @DocItemId = 200,    @WHId = 2,    @UserId = 3,    @Month1 = 11,   @FPId = 30,    @RegisterDate = '2022-01-24',   @RegisterDatePersian = '14001104',   @AppType = 'dasti',   @LanguageID = 1065  */  @DocItemId INT,   @WHId INT,   @UserId INT,   @Month1 INT,  @FPId INT,   @RegisterDate DATETIME,  @RegisterDatePersian VARCHAR(8),  @AppType NVARCHAR(50),  @LanguageID INT = NULL  )  AS  BEGIN  /**/  /*--------------------------------------------*/  /*------------ Define variables --------------*/  DECLARE @Quantity FLOAT, @PrdId VARCHAR(50),@SerialCount INT,@DocId INT    SELECT          @PrdId = [PrdId]        ,@Quantity = [Quantity]     ,@DocId = [DocId]  FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems]  WHERE [DocItemID] = @DocItemId    SET @SerialCount = (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials] WHERE [ItemId] = @DocItemId)    SET @Quantity = ISNULL(@Quantity,0) - ISNULL(@SerialCount,0)    /*--------------------------------------------*/  /*Find oprId*/  DECLARE @SelectSerialByWhId INT, @OprType INT    SET @OprType = (SELECT TOP (1) [OprType]      FROM [dbo].[WSDocOpr]      WHERE [DocOprID] = (SELECT TOP (1) [OprId]            FROM [dbo].[WSDoc]            WHERE [DocID] = @DocId))  IF(ISNULL(@OprType,0) = 2)  BEGIN   SET @SelectSerialByWhId = @WHId  END  ELSE IF(ISNULL(@OprType,0) = 3)  BEGIN   SET @SelectSerialByWhId = (SELECT TOP (1) [WHIdI] FROM [dbo].[WSDoc] WHERE [DocID] = @DocId)  END  ELSE   BEGIN   SET @SelectSerialByWhId = 0  END  /*--------------------------------------------*/     INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials]          ([ItemId]          ,[Serial]          ,[WHId]          ,[UserId]          ,[Month1]          ,[FPId]          ,[RegisterDate]          ,[RegisterDatePersian]          ,[SerialId]          ,[AppType]          ,[LanguageID]          ,[FirstRegisterUserId]          ,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]          ,[FirstRegisterDate]          ,[RootId])      SELECT TOP(CAST(@Quantity AS BIGINT))     @DocItemId    ,DIS.[Serial]    ,@WHId    ,@UserId    ,@Month1    ,@FPId    ,@RegisterDate    ,@RegisterDatePersian    ,DIS.[DocItemSerialID]    ,@AppType    ,@LanguageID    ,@UserId    ,@RegisterDatePersian    ,@RegisterDate    ,(CASE WHEN (ISNULL(DIS.[RootId],0) <> 0) THEN DIS.[RootId] ELSE DIS.[DocItemSerialID] END)   FROM dbo.WSDocItemSerials DIS INNER JOIN dbo.WSDocItems DI      ON(DIS.ItemId = DI.DocItemID)      INNER JOIN dbo.WSDoc D    ON(DI.DocId = D.DocID)   WHERE OprId IN (SELECT DocOprID FROM dbo.WSDocOpr WHERE OprType IN (1,3))   And DI.PrdId = @PrdId     And D.WHId = @SelectSerialByWhId   AND DIS.DocItemSerialID NOT IN (SELECT ISNULL(DISI.SerialId, '') FROM dbo.WSDocItemSerials DISI)   AND DIS.DocItemSerialID NOT IN (SELECT OIS.[SerialId] FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItemSerials] OIS)   ORDER BY DIS .ExpDate, DIS .DocItemSerialID  /*-------------------------------------*/  /**/  END                  GO

-- [dbo].[MobspInsertDocItemFromLinkedPrd]              /*AI14020525 - Copied from web*/  /*AI14020912 - Calculate RateResult in fiexd PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto*/  CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspInsertDocItemFromLinkedPrd]  (    /*   EXEC MobspInsertDocItemFromLinkedPrd    @ProductId = 7927,   @DocId = 551,     @WHId  = 2,   @UserId = 3,   @FPId = 1,   @Month1 = 12,   @RegisterDate = '2021-08-15',   @RegisterDatePersian = '14000524',   @AppType = 'dasti',   @LanguageID = 1065,   @DefaultLanguageID = 1065,   @PrdStockTxt = NULL  */  @ProductId INT,  @DocId INT,   @WHId INT,  @UserId INT,  @FPId INT,  @Month1 INT,  @RegisterDate DATETIME,  @RegisterDatePersian VARCHAR(8),  @AppType NVARCHAR(20),  @LanguageID INT,  @DefaultLanguageID INT,  @PrdStockTxt NVARCHAR(100) = NULL  )  AS  BEGIN  /**/  /*-------------------------- Begin define variables -----------------------*/  DECLARE @LinkedProductId INT,@LinkedPrdId INT,  @LinkedPrdQuantity FLOAT,@LinkedPrdNotVisible BIT,  @LinkedPrdNotInSum BIT,@LinkedPrdAddQuantity BIT,  @LinkedPrdAddPrice BIT,  @PrdDescription NVARCHAR(MAX),  @prdUnitId INT,@DocItemId INT,  @FCR FLOAT,@FCRI FLOAT,@Quantity FLOAT,  @MainDocIQuantity FLOAT,  @QuantityIn FLOAT,   @QuantityOut FLOAT,   @RemainCount FLOAT,@OprType INT,  @ErrorMessage NVARCHAR(MAX) = '', @CalcQtyWhId INT  ,@PrdUnitIdI INT,@PrdUnitIRate FLOAT,  @PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto BIT,@PrdQtyNoFCR BIT,  @PrdUnitIRateResult FLOAT,@TmpI FLOAT  ,@PrdNickname NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL  /*--------------------------------------------------------------------------*/  IF(ISNULL(@PrdStockTxt,'') = '')  BEGIN  /**/   SET @PrdStockTxt = N'موجودی محصول'  /**/  END    SET @MainDocIQuantity = (SELECT TOP 1 Quantity FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems]           WHERE DocId = @DocId           AND PrdId = @ProductId          AND WHId = @WHId)  SET @OprType = (SELECT TOP 1        [OprType]           FROM [dbo].[WSDocOpr]       WHERE [DocOprID] = (SELECT TOP 1             [OprId]            FROM [dbo].[WSDoc]            WHERE [DocID] = @DocId))  SET @CalcQtyWhId = (SELECT TOP 1         [WhId]        FROM [dbo].[WSDoc]        WHERE [DocID] = @DocId)  IF(ISNULL(@OprType,0) = 3)  BEGIN   SET @CalcQtyWhId = (SELECT TOP 1          [WhIdI] /*calculate in source warehouse*/       FROM [dbo].[WSDoc]        WHERE [DocID] = @DocId)  END  /*-------------------------- End define variables --------------------------*/  /*--------------------------------------------------------------------------*/  BEGIN TRY   /*--------------------------------------*/   BEGIN TRANSACTION   /*--------------------------------------*/   DECLARE Crsr_Add_Item CURSOR LOCAL FOR     SELECT       [LinkedProductId]            ,(CASE WHEN PrdId = @ProductId          THEN LinkedPrdId         ELSE PrdId               END) [LinkedPrdId]         ,[LinkedPrdQuantity]     ,[LinkedPrdNotVisible]     ,[LinkedPrdNotInSum]     ,[LinkedPrdAddQuantity]     ,[LinkedPrdAddPrice]    FROM [dbo].[WSLinkedProducts]     WHERE ([PrdId] = @ProductId     OR  [LinkedPrdId] = @ProductId)           OPEN Crsr_Add_Item   FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Add_Item INTO @LinkedProductId,@LinkedPrdId,              @LinkedPrdQuantity,@LinkedPrdNotVisible,              @LinkedPrdNotInSum,@LinkedPrdAddQuantity,              @LinkedPrdAddPrice   WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0   BEGIN    /*--------------------------------------------*/    SET @DocItemId = NULL        SET @PrdDescription = NULL      SET @prdUnitId = NULL      SET @FCR = NULL    SET @FCRI = NULL    SET @Quantity = NULL    SET @QuantityIn = NULL    SET @QuantityOut = NULL     SET @RemainCount = NULL     SET @PrdUnitIdI = NULL    SET @PrdUnitIRate = NULL    SET @PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto = NULL    SET @PrdQtyNoFCR = NULL    SET @PrdUnitIRateResult = NULL    SET @TmpI = NULL    SET @PrdNickname = NULL    /*-------------- Set variables -------------------*/      SELECT      @PrdDescription = PrdDescription,     @prdUnitId = prdUnitId,     @FCR = (SELECT FCR FROM [dbo].[WSProductUnits] WHERE ProductUnitId = prdUnitId),     @FCRI = (SELECT FCRI FROM [dbo].[WSProductUnits] WHERE ProductUnitId = prdUnitId),     @PrdUnitIdI = [PrdUnitIdI],     @PrdUnitIRate = [PrdUnitIRate],     @PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto = [PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto],     @PrdQtyNoFCR = [PrdQtyNoFCR],     @PrdNickname = [PrdNickname]    FROM [dbo].[WSProducts]    WHERE ProductId = @LinkedPrdId      /**/    SET @Quantity = ISNULL(@LinkedPrdQuantity,1)    IF(ISNULL(@LinkedPrdAddQuantity,0) = 1)    BEGIN    /**/     SET @Quantity = ISNULL(@Quantity,1) * ISNULL(@MainDocIQuantity,1)    /**/    END    /*-------------------------------------------*/           /*----------- Calculate Stock ---------------*/    SET @QuantityIn = (SELECT           ISNULL(SUM(DI.Quantity), 0)         FROM dbo.WSDoc D          INNER JOIN dbo.WSDocItems DI ON(D.DocID = DI.DocId)         WHERE OprId IN (SELECT DocOprId FROM dbo.WSDocOpr WHERE OprType IN (1,3) )         And DI.PrdId = @LinkedPrdId          And D.WHId = @CalcQtyWhId                GROUP BY DI.PrdId)    /**/    SET @QuantityOut = (SELECT            ISNULL(SUM(DI.Quantity), 0)         FROM dbo.WSDoc D          INNER JOIN dbo.WSDocItems DI ON(D.DocID = DI.DocId)         WHERE OprId IN (SELECT DocOprId FROM dbo.WSDocOpr WHERE OprType = 2)         And DI.PrdId = @LinkedPrdId         And D.WHId = @CalcQtyWhId                OR (           (OprId IN (SELECT DocOprId FROM dbo.WSDocOpr WHERE OprType = 3))           And DI.PrdId = @LinkedPrdId          And D.WHIdI = @CalcQtyWhId                 )         GROUP BY DI.PrdId)    /**/    SET @RemainCount = ISNULL(@QuantityIn, 0) - ISNULL(@QuantityOut, 0)    /**/    IF(ISNULL(@OprType,0) = 2 OR ISNULL(@OprType,0) = 3)    BEGIN    /**/     IF(ISNULL(@RemainCount,0) < ISNULL(@Quantity,1))     BEGIN     /**/      /*----------------------------------------*/      /*Get prdName*/      IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)      BEGIN      /**/       SET @ErrorMessage = ISNULL(@ErrorMessage,'') +              (SELECT TOP 1             [PrdName]                   FROM dbo.WSProducts P            WHERE [ProductID] = @LinkedPrdId) + N' ('              + @PrdStockTxt + N' = ' + CAST(ISNULL(@RemainCount,0) AS NVARCHAR(50))+ N')' + N'|'      /**/      END      ELSE IF(@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)      BEGIN      /**/       SET @ErrorMessage = ISNULL(@ErrorMessage,'') +              (SELECT TOP 1             [PrdName]                   FROM dbo.WSProducts_Lang P            WHERE [ProductID] = @LinkedPrdId            AND P.LanguageID = @LanguageID)              + @PrdStockTxt + N' = ' + CAST(ISNULL(@RemainCount,0) AS NVARCHAR(50))+ N')' + N'|'           /**/      END        /*----------------------------------------*/      FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Add_Item INTO @LinkedProductId,@LinkedPrdId,               @LinkedPrdQuantity,@LinkedPrdNotVisible,               @LinkedPrdNotInSum,@LinkedPrdAddQuantity,               @LinkedPrdAddPrice      CONTINUE     /**/     END    /**/    END      /*-------------------------------------------*/     SET @TmpI = ISNULL(@Quantity,1) * ISNULL(@FCR,1) * ISNULL(@FCRI,1)    IF(ISNULL(@PrdQtyNoFCR,0) = 1)    BEGIN    /**/     SET @TmpI = ISNULL(@Quantity,1)    /**/    END    /*----------------------------------------------------*/        IF(/*ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto,0) = 1 AND*/ ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRate,0) <> 0) /*AI14020912- Calculate RateResult in fiexd PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto*/    BEGIN    /**/     SET @PrdUnitIRateResult = (1 / ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRate,0)) * ISNULL(@Quantity,1)       IF(ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRateResult,0) <> 0)     BEGIN     /**/      SET @PrdUnitIRateResult = CAST((FORMAT(@PrdUnitIRateResult, '000.000')) AS FLOAT)      /**/     END    /**/    END      /*-------------------------------------------*/     /*Insert WSDocItems*/    INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSDocItems]     ([DocId]     ,[PrdId]     ,[Quantity]        ,[Description]     ,[WHId]     ,[UserId]     ,[Month1]     ,[FPId]     ,[RegisterDate]     ,[RegisterDatePersian]     ,[NotVisible]     ,[AppType]     ,[LanguageID]     ,[FirstRegisterUserId]     ,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]     ,[FirstRegisterDate]     ,[FCR]     ,[FCRI]     ,[PrdUnitId]     ,[PrdUnitIdI]     ,[PrdUnitIRateResult]     ,[PrdNickname]     )    VALUES     (@DocId     ,@LinkedPrdId     ,ISNULL(@Quantity,1)     ,@PrdDescription        ,@WHId     ,@UserId     ,@Month1     ,@FPId     ,@RegisterDate     ,@RegisterDatePersian     ,@LinkedPrdNotVisible     ,@AppType     ,@DefaultLanguageID     ,@UserId     ,@RegisterDatePersian     ,@RegisterDate     ,@FCR     ,@FCRI     ,@prdUnitId     ,@PrdUnitIdI     ,@PrdUnitIRateResult     ,@PrdNickname     )          /*-------------------------------------------*/     SET @DocItemId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()    /*-------------------------------------------*/    IF(ISNULL(@DocItemId,0) > 0)    BEGIN    /**/     /*-------------------------------------------*/     /*Insert records in lang table*/     INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSDocItems_Lang] (       DocItemID,       LanguageID,       [Description],       [PrdNickname]     )      SELECT      @DocItemId,      PLN.LanguageID,      PrdDescription,      PrdNickname     FROM dbo.WSProducts_Lang PLN      WHERE  PLN.ProductID = @LinkedPrdId              /*-------------------------------------------*/    /**/    END      /*--------------------------------------------*/    FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Add_Item INTO @LinkedProductId,@LinkedPrdId,               @LinkedPrdQuantity,@LinkedPrdNotVisible,               @LinkedPrdNotInSum,@LinkedPrdAddQuantity,               @LinkedPrdAddPrice   END   CLOSE Crsr_Add_Item   DEALLOCATE Crsr_Add_Item     /*-------------------------------*/   SELECT 1 AS Result,@ErrorMessage 'ErrorMessage'     COMMIT  /*--------------------------------------*/  END TRY  BEGIN CATCH      IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0          ROLLBACK      /*CLOSE Crsr_Add_Item   DEALLOCATE Crsr_Add_Item*/   SELECT 0 AS Result,N'Error' 'ErrorMessage'    END CATCH  /*--------------------------------------------------------------------------*/  /**/    END                GO

[PrdQtyNoFCR],      @PrdNickname = [PrdNickname]     FROM [dbo].[WSProducts]     WHERE ProductId = @LinkedPrdId       /**/     SET @Quantity = ISNULL(@LinkedPrdQuantity,1)          /*----------- Calculate Stock ---------------*/     SET @QuantityIn = (SELECT            ISNULL(SUM(DI.Quantity), 0)          FROM dbo.WSDoc D           INNER JOIN dbo.WSDocItems DI ON(D.DocID = DI.DocId)          WHERE OprId IN (SELECT DocOprId FROM dbo.WSDocOpr WHERE OprType IN (1,3) )          And DI.PrdId = @LinkedPrdId           And D.WHId = @CalcQtyWhId 0)     BEGIN     /**/      /*-------------------------------------------*/      /*Insert records in lang table*/      INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSDocItems_Lang] (        DocItemID,        LanguageID,        [Description],        PrdNickname      )       SELECT       @InsertedDocItemId,       PLN.LanguageID,       PrdDescription,       PrdNickname      FROM dbo.WSProducts_Lang PLN       WHERE  PLN.ProductID = @LinkedPrdId               /*-------------------------------------------*/     /**/     END       /*--------------------------------------------*/     FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Add_Item INTO @LinkedProductId,@LinkedPrdId,                @LinkedPrdQuantity,@LinkedPrdNotVisible,                @LinkedPrdNotInSum,@LinkedPrdAddQuantity,                @LinkedPrdAddPrice    END    CLOSE Crsr_Add_Item    DEALLOCATE Crsr_Add_Item      /*-------------------------------*/    SELECT 1 AS Result     COMMIT   /*--------------------------------------*/   END TRY   BEGIN CATCH    IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0     ROLLBACK    /*CLOSE Crsr_Add_Item    DEALLOCATE Crsr_Add_Item*/    SELECT 0 AS Result     END CATCH      /*---------------------------------------------*/  END  ELSE  BEGIN  /**/        ,@RegisterDatePersian          ,@AppType          ,@LanguageID          ,@UserId          ,@RegisterDatePersian          ,@RegisterDate          ,0)        SET @InsertedDocISerialId =        END          /*ELSE IF(@RootId <> 0)          BEGIN           SET @RootId = @RootId          END*/          /*----------------*/          /*RZ14000325- End*/ ,@RegisterDate           ,@SerialI           ,@RootId           )         SET @InsertedDocISerialId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()        END       END                /*********** Insert to lang table -- [dbo].[MobspInsertUpdateTimeAttendance]            /*AI14020121*/  CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspInsertUpdateTimeAttendance]  (   /*    EXEC [MobspInsertUpdateTimeAttendance]  */  @Mode INT,  @TimeAttendanceId INT = NULL,  @tmDate DATETIME,  @tmDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),  @tmUserId INT,  @tmEntryTime DATETIME = NULL,  @tmEntryRegisterIP NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,  @tmExitTime DATETIME = NULL,  @tmExitRegisterIP NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,  @tmDescription NVARCHAR(300) = NULL,  /**/  @UserId INT,  @Year1 SMALLINT,  @Month1 SMALLINT,  @RegisterDate DATETIME,  @RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),  @AppType VARCHAR(20),   @LanguageID INT = NULL,  @DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL,  @tmEntryTimeVerifyUserId INT = NULL,  @tmEntryTimeVerifyDate DATETIME = NULL,  @tmEntryTimeVerifyDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,  @tmExitTimeVerifyUserId INT = NULL,  @tmExitTimeVerifyDate DATETIME = NULL,  @tmExitTimeVerifyDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,  /**/  @Result INT OUTPUT  )  AS   BEGIN  /**/  BEGIN TRY     DECLARE @EntryBase DATETIME = NULL     ,@EntryDelay INT = NULL     ,@EntryDelayAllowMin INT = NULL     ,@ExitBase DATETIME = NULL     ,@ExitHurryAllowMin INT = NULL     ,@ExitHurry INT = NULL     IF(@tmEntryTime IS NOT NULL)   BEGIN    SET @EntryBase = CONVERT(DATETIME,     CAST(YEAR( @tmDate) AS VARCHAR(10))+'-'+     CAST(MONTH( @tmDate) AS VARCHAR(10))+'-'+     CAST(DAY( @tmDate) AS VARCHAR(10))+' '+     (SELECT tmbEntryBase FROM dbo.CrmTimeAttendanceBase       WHERE tmbDay = (SELECT DATEPART( dw, @tmDate)))      )      SET @EntryDelay = (SELECT DATEDIFF(mi, @EntryBase, @tmEntryTime))      SET @EntryDelayAllowMin = (SELECT tmbEntryAllowMin FROM dbo.CrmTimeAttendanceBase WHERE tmbDay = (SELECT            DATEPART( dw, @tmDate)))   END       IF(@tmExitTime IS NOT NULL)   BEGIN    SET @ExitBase = CONVERT(DATETIME,     CAST(YEAR( @tmDate) AS VARCHAR(10)) +'-'+     CAST(MONTH( @tmDate) AS VARCHAR(10)) +'-'+     CAST(DAY( @tmDate) AS VARCHAR(10)) +' '+     (SELECT tmbExitBase FROM dbo.CrmTimeAttendanceBase       WHERE tmbDay = (SELECT DATEPART( dw, @tmDate)))      )      SET @ExitHurryAllowMin = (SELECT tmbExitAllowMin FROM dbo.CrmTimeAttendanceBase      WHERE tmbDay = (SELECT DATEPART( dw, @tmDate)))      SET @ExitHurry = -(SELECT DATEDIFF(mi, @ExitBase, @tmExitTime) )   END        IF(ISNULL(@TimeAttendanceId, 0) <> 0)   BEGIN /*Update*/   /**/    UPDATE [dbo].[CrmTimeAttendance] SET     [tmDate] = @tmDate     ,[tmEntryBase] = @EntryBase     ,[tmEntryTime] = @tmEntryTime     ,[tmEntryDelay] = @EntryDelay     ,[tmEntryDelayAllowMin] = @EntryDelayAllowMin     ,[tmEntryRegisterIP] = @tmEntryRegisterIP     ,[tmExitBase] = @ExitBase     ,[tmExitTime] = @tmExitTime     ,[tmExitHurry] = @ExitHurry     ,[tmExitHurryAllowMin] = @ExitHurryAllowMin     ,[tmExitRegisterIP] = @tmExitRegisterIP     ,[UserId] = @UserId     ,[RegisterDate] = @RegisterDate     ,[RegisterDatePersian] = @RegisterDatePersian     ,[AppType] = @AppType     ,[tmEntryTimeVerifyUserId] = @tmEntryTimeVerifyUserId     ,[tmEntryTimeVerifyDate] = @tmEntryTimeVerifyDate     ,[tmEntryTimeVerifyDatePersian] = @tmEntryTimeVerifyDatePersian     ,[tmExitTimeVerifyUserId] = @tmExitTimeVerifyUserId     ,[tmExitTimeVerifyDate] = @tmExitTimeVerifyDate     ,[tmExitTimeVerifyDatePersian] = @tmExitTimeVerifyDatePersian      WHERE [TimeAttendanceId] -- [dbo].[MobspInternalMessageUpdate]              /*AI991127*/  CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspInternalMessageUpdate]  /*   exec MobspInternalMessageUpdate 'Read', 10597, 3, 10, '13:01:22', '13991127', 'Mob'  */  (  @Mode VARCHAR(50),  @MessageID INT,  @UserID INT,  @TypeID INT,  @Time VARCHAR(20),  @Date VARCHAR(8),  @AppType NVARCHAR(50)   )  As  BEGIN   IF @Mode = 'Receive'   BEGIN     UPDATE dbo.CrmInternalMessageDetails     SET msgdReceiveTime = @Time     ,msgdReceiveDate = @Date     ,msgdReceiveDateTime = GETDATE()     ,AppType = @AppType     WHERE 1=1    AND msgdToUserId = @UserID    AND msgdReceiveTime IS NULL   END   /**/   IF @Mode = 'Read'   BEGIN     UPDATE dbo.CrmInternalMessageDetails    SET msgdIsRead = 1     ,msgdReadTime = @Time     ,msgdReadDate = @Date     ,msgdReadDateTime = GETDATE()     ,AppType = @AppType     WHERE msgdInternalMessageId = @MessageID    AND msgdToUserId = @UserID    AND msgdTypeId = @TypeID    AND msgdIsRead <> 1   END   /**/   IF @Mode = 'UnRead'   BEGIN     UPDATE dbo.CrmInternalMessageDetails    SET msgdIsRead = 0     ,msgdReadTime = @Time     ,msgdReadDate = @Date     ,msgdReadDateTime = GETDATE()     ,AppType = @AppType     WHERE msgdInternalMessageId = @MessageID    AND msgdToUserId = @UserID    AND msgdTypeId = @TypeID    AND msgdIsRead <> 0   END  END              GO

@FieldName NVARCHAR(200)        SET @FieldName = SUBSTRING(@Field, 1, CHARINDEX('=',@Field)-1)                                            SET @FieldValue = SUBSTRING(@Field, CHARINDEX('=', @Field)+1, Len(@Field))        /*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/        /*---------------------------- BEGIN Fill variabele ------------------------------*/        /*BEGIN*/        IF @FieldName = 'LangId'        BEGIN         SET @LangId = CASE WHEN CAST(ISNULL(@FieldValue,'0') AS INT) = 0 THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@FieldValue AS INT) END        END        /*--------------------------*/        IF @FieldName = 'Txt'        BEGIN         SET @Txt = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@FieldValue,'') = '' THEN NULL ELSE @FieldValue END        END        /*--------------------------*/        IF @FieldName = 'Subj'        BEGIN         SET @Subj = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@FieldValue,'') = '' THEN NULL ELSE @FieldValue END        END        /*--------------------------*/        IF @FieldName = 'VerifiedSubj'        BEGIN         SET @VerifiedSubj = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@FieldValue,'') = '' THEN NULL ELSE @FieldValue END        END        /*--------------------------*/            /*END */        /*---------------------------- END Fill variabele --------------------------------*/        /*---------------------------- Remove current parameter from listparam ------------*/        SET @Text = SUBSTRING(@Text,LEN(@Field + '|,|') + 1,LEN(@Text))       END       ELSE       BEGIN        SET @Field = @Text        SET @Text = NULL              END      END      /*===========================================================*/      INSERT INTO @TmpTable ([LangId],Txt,Subj,VerifiedSubj)      VALUES(@LangId,@Txt,@Subj,@VerifiedSubj)     /**/     END     /*================================================================================*/                       /**/     FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr INTO @Text    END    CLOSE Crsr    DEALLOCATE Crsr   /**/   END     /*============================= Insert into WSVerificationRequestDetails ===================*/    DECLARE @VerificationRequestDetailId INT   INSERT INTO ,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]             ,[UserId]             ,[Year1]             ,[Month1]             ,[RegisterDate]             ,[RegisterDatePersian]             ,[AppType]             ,[LanguageID])      VALUES             (@VerificationRequestId             ,@ObjectId             ,@ObjectType             ,@VerificationWorkFlowId             ,@VerificationWorkFlowStepId             ,@UserId             ,@RegisterDate             ,@VerifyText             ,@UserId             ,@RegisterDate             ,@RegisterDatePersian             ,@UserId             ,@FPId             ,@Month1             ,@RegisterDate             ,@RegisterDatePersian             ,@AppType             ,@DefaultLanguageID)   SET @VerificationRequestDetailId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
